In reset_tree.py on rt aa4d91420554, lock is read only through pad_spec(), which returns None unless reset.source.pad is set; and a lock needs {inst, pin} of a PLL lock, while QC clocks are pins (no PLL; RESET-HOLD-SWEEP 01:51 also found no lockq in the bound QC root).
